### F-080 · Engine-vs-TradingView OHLC agreement is exact everywhere except the daily-open bar — 30/30 divergences localize to one slot per trading day, a cross-feed session-boundary artifact, not an engine or aggregation defect
- Type:          ARCHITECTURE
- Family:        — (measurement/data-lineage property; see docs/governance/research_family_registry.json)
- Contract:      UNKNOWN
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Certain
- Validated:     2026-08-17
- Revalidate-by: 2026-11-15
- Evidence:      `CORRECTED 2026-08-18 (CH-xauusd-tv-odds, user-authorized) — the original counts in this block were computed by SUMMING per-shot totals across OVERLAPPING shot windows and are superseded by the deduplicated figures below; the original numbers are preserved inline (§6.2 rule 4), not deleted.` First month-wide engine↔TradingView reconciliation on XAUUSD (`data/XAUUSD_M15.csv`, 2,116 bars, 2026-07-07 01:00 → 2026-08-06 23:45 broker), across **10 captured shots** at two timeframes. `CORRECTED: 1,455 compared / 1,430 MATCH / 25 DIVERGENT (98.3%) -> 1,378 compared / 1,357 MATCH / 21 DIVERGENT (98.5%)` — the same 10 shots deduplicated on `(timeframe, broker-timestamp)`; per-timeframe M15 1,241/9 and H4 137/12. Threshold unchanged: the inherited `engine_data.MATCH_TOLERANCE=3.0`. **Why the original was inflated:** `scripts/research/htf_parent_telemetry_extract.py:163-164` sums each sidecar's `compared`/`divergent` without deduplicating, and the windows overlap — `05_m15_jul28_displacement` (13) and `06_m15_jul30_trade` (6) are strict subsets of `04_m15_jul28_forensic` (202), and both `03_h4_jul27_31` and `09_h4_jul15_20` sit inside `01_h4_july_macro`. Arithmetic: 202+13+6+82+37+552+368 = 1,260 summed M15 vs **1,241 unique**, +195 H4 = exactly 1,455. All 4 phantom divergences were H4, contributed by the two close-ups re-reporting bars the macro shot had already counted. That is a valid per-capture work tally — which is all the telemetry script claims — but not a valid denominator for an agreement RATE. **Full-coverage extension (2026-08-18):** three new M15 shots (`12_m15_jul15_20`, `13_m15_jul20_28`, `14_m15_jul30_31`) took M15 coverage from 1,241 to **2,116 of 2,116 corpus bars (100.0%)**; 13 shots, all resolving UTC+3 decisively (0.372 vs runner-up 30.094). Deduplicated totals become **2,253 compared / 30 DIVERGENT** (2,419/34 if summed naively). M15 **2,098/2,116 agree = 99.15%**, 1 divergent in 117.6, Wilson 95% CI on divergence 0.54–1.34%; H4 **125/137 = 91.24%**, 1 in 11.4, CI 5.08–14.69%. The divergences are not scattered — `CORRECTED: 25 of 25 -> 30 of 30 fall on the daily-open slot` (H4 broker `00:00` `CORRECTED: ×16 -> ×12`, M15 broker `01:00` `CORRECTED: ×9 -> ×18`) and **zero at any other time of day** — and the ~875 newly measured M15 bars span every hour, which made that localization claim genuinely falsifiable for the first time rather than an artifact of where captures happened to cluster. **Away from the 01:00 slot the two feeds agree on 2,093 of 2,093 M15 bars**, with a maximum open-field disagreement of 0.45. Cross-shot consistency CLEAN: on all 175 bars covered by more than one independently-framed shot, engine OHLC, TradingView OHLC and verdict agree exactly (0 conflicts). Producer `scripts/research/tv_engine_odds.py`, artifacts `reports/xauusd_month_tv_engine_odds.md` + `results/xauusd_tv_odds/xauusd_tv_engine_odds.json` (idempotent, byte-identical on re-run). at H4 every one of the 9 daily-open buckets in the macro shot shows an elevated open delta while all 5 other buckets per day (04/08/12/16/20) sit at ≈0.06–1.6 L1. MECHANISM (source- and data-verified): the engine corpus opens its trading day at 01:00 broker — `2026-07-31` runs `01:00..23:45`, and the 00:00/00:15/00:30/00:45 slots have no bars at all — while OANDA opens earlier, so the engine's first print of the day lands after OANDA has already traded the session gap; the H4 `00:00` bucket inherits that open because its only existing child is the 01:00 bar. Field attribution is NOT universal — and at full coverage it is no longer even a majority: `CORRECTED 2026-08-18: the open dominates the L1 error in 14 of 25 (56%) -> 15 of 30 (exactly 50%)`, with High co-equal at 13 of 30 (M15 alone: High 9, Open 7, Low 2). The original 56% was itself stated as measured and deliberately not rounded up to "always the open"; the correction moves it below the majority line, so the claim is now that the open is *one of two* dominant fields, not the dominant one. H4 was reconcilable at all only because `tools/tv_forensic/htf_bars.py` (new) aggregates via the canonical `features.parent_candle.ParentCandleBuilder` rather than re-deriving HTF geometry, and resolves the grid PHASE by measurement: each candidate phase re-aggregates the children, so a shifted TradingView grid would be discovered rather than merely rejected. Result — **phase 0, decisive, all 137 in-frame parents matched**: TradingView's H4 bars land on UTC 17/21/01/05/09/13, which at the measured +3 offset is broker 20/00/04/08/12/16, exactly the calendar-true grid. Fails closed to `NOT_APPLICABLE`/`H4_GRID_UNRESOLVED` otherwise. Artifacts: `reports/monthly_tv_vs_active_production_semantic_comparison.{md,json}`, `results/monthly_tv_semantic_report/ohlc_clock_reconciliation.json` (`unreconciled_shots: []`), `tools/tv_forensic/shots/01_h4_july_macro.json`, floors in `tests/test_tv_forensic_smoke.py` (`CORRECTED: 36 -> 44 tests`, the 8 added pinning the deduplicating unit of account so the naive sum cannot return; the overlap floor is mutation-verified to FAIL against a naive-sum implementation).
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      —
- Owner:         claude (user-authorized 2026-08-17, CH-monthly-tv-coverage-h4-recon)
- Note:          **Pre-registered, and the tolerance was NOT tuned to the answer.** `docs/governance/build_manifests/CH-monthly-tv-coverage-h4-recon.impact.json` fixed the verdict rules before any capture ran: inherit `MATCH_TOLERANCE=3.0` unchanged, fail closed on an unresolved grid, count pre-corpus slots as `NO_ENGINE_BAR` in the denominator rather than dropping them, and register a new F-id ONLY IF divergence beyond tolerance appeared. It did, so this is registered; a clean result would have registered nothing. Widening the tolerance afterwards would have made all 25 vanish and was explicitly not done — 4 of 9 daily opens cross it and 5 do not, and that split is a property of gap sizes, not of a chosen threshold. **NOT a defect claim (§6.8):** `different ≠ wrong`. Two feeds with different session-open instants MUST disagree on the first bar of the day; the repo's own `tools/tv_forensic/shot_plan.json` already records the same class of feed difference ("the 07:45 bar low is 4044.48 on the engine feed but 4044.34 on OANDA, so exit timing is feed-dependent"). No verdict on any prior finding changes: F-075's M15 "0 divergent bars" over shots 07/08 reproduces exactly, and shot 09 — which F-079 correctly recorded as never reconciled — now reconciles (28 bars, 2 divergent, both daily-open), resolving that gap rather than reversing it. **Coverage context:** TradingView coverage of the corpus month went from 5 of ~22 trading days to **23 of 23**; 1 of 11 planned shots (`02_h4_july_setup`) remains uncaptured because `frame_shot`'s two-sided assertion refuses a right edge ~2 days wider than requested on BOTH the widget-API and `--via-ui` paths — the guard was deliberately not loosened, and the window is a strict subset of the captured `01_h4_july_macro`, so no coverage is lost. Root cause of that framing behaviour is UNRESOLVED and left open. Grants **no G001, no production authority, no promotion**; `ACTIVE_VERSION` unchanged (`v2_htfcrt_2026_08`); CRT closure stays REOPENED. Does not adjudicate F-077's Romeo/Sujan non-equivalence — better coverage is not a semantic equivalence proof. **Close-field refinement (2026-08-17, same sidecars, no new capture):** composite L1 is the wrong instrument for the object `process_candle` consumes. On **1,241 unique M15 bars** (15 of 23 corpus days; H4 shots do not measure M15 Close), Close `\|TV−engine\|` max = **0.99**, **0 bars > 1.0**, **0 bars > 3.0**. The 9 M15 composite-`DIVERGENT` bars are all `01:00`; their Close max is **0.195**. `CORRECTED 2026-08-18 at full coverage (2,116 M15 bars, 23 of 23 days): Close max |TV−engine| 0.99 -> 2.45, bars > 1.0: 0 -> 2` (still **0 bars > 3.0**, so neither newly-seen bar is composite-`DIVERGENT`, and both sit AWAY from the daily open); on the now-18 M15 composite-`DIVERGENT` bars the `Close max is 0.195 -> 0.355`. The qualitative claim is unchanged and in fact sharpened: Close is the field that AGREES — the two feeds diverge at the open and reconverge within the same bar. Two session-end `23:45` bars are the only Close residuals above 0.5 (0.990, 0.545) and remain `OK`. This is M15 Close **MATCH**, not a new F-id and not a reversal of the 25 L1 flags. It does **not** assign HTF any predictive or qualification role. **Count correction + full-coverage extension (2026-08-18, `CH-xauusd-tv-odds`, user-authorized; no new F-id — a tidier number is not a discovery).** Two defects blocked this finding from answering "what are the odds these two feeds agree." (1) The denominator was a NAIVE SUM over overlapping shot windows, so 1,455/25 counted 77 bars and 4 divergences twice; deduplicated on `(timeframe, broker-timestamp)` the same 10 shots give **1,378/21**. (2) M15 coverage was 1,241 of 2,116 corpus bars (58.6%), so the localization claim rested on a few clustered spans. Three new M15 shots took coverage to **100%**, adding ~875 bars across every hour of the day — and the claim held **30/30**. What SURVIVED: the localization conclusion, the session-boundary mechanism, the calendar-true H4 grid (phase 0, decisive), and the §6.8 `different ≠ wrong` framing. What MOVED: the counts, the H4/M15 slot split, open-field dominance (56% → 50%, below majority), and the Close max (0.99 → 2.45). **This is a CORRECTION, not a reversal** — every original figure is preserved inline and marked `CORRECTED` per §6.2 rule 4, and the finding's conclusion, status and confidence are unchanged. The root cause is a reusable lesson, not a one-off typo: a per-capture *work* tally and a *rate* denominator are different objects, and reusing the first as the second inflates silently — now pinned by 8 floors in `tests/test_tv_forensic_smoke.py`, one of them mutation-verified to fail against the naive sum. Grants **no** new authority; still no G001, no promotion, `ACTIVE_VERSION` unchanged, CRT stays REOPENED.

### F-081 · The Visual CRT trade object (chart-visible pool -> sweep -> F-074 directional displacement) carries NO directional edge on XAUUSD — both pre-registered arms return a well-powered gross expectancy indistinguishable from zero, under two opposite payoff geometries
- Type:          ECONOMIC
- Family:        — (new pool-founded ontology; see docs/governance/research_family_registry.json)
- Contract:      MC-VCRT-XAUUSD-M15-V1
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Likely
- Validated:     2026-08-17
- Revalidate-by: 2026-11-15
- Evidence:      First measurement of SEM-012 (`VISUAL_CRT_TRADE_OBJECT`, v2), the object built to answer "how many profitable trades are on the TradingView screenshots" after that question was found unanswerable (across 10 shots exactly 1 of 19 marks is trade-shaped, it is SUPERSEDED under F-074, and it lost at 0.09R MFE; the engine's own month ledger is `84 SWEEP -> 2 BEGIN_SOFT_CONF -> 2 FILTER_REJECTED -> 0 TRADE_OPENED`). Founded deliberately NOT on SEM-011 M15-SLR but on **chart-visible pools** (prior CLOSED calendar H4 high/low, PDH/PDL) — a genuinely new founding structure, which is F-028's only legitimate reopen condition for the directional class. Corpus: `data/mt5/XAUUSD_M15.csv`, **47,275 bars**, 2024-05-22 -> 2026-05-21, sha256 `4d73f5ce…`; L3 `dataset_integrity.validate_dataset` APPROVE (504 intra-session gaps, 0 missing candles). Exit `forward_walk(intrabar_fixed)`, 40-bar horizon, TP `2.0*atr_abs`, SL beyond the swept extreme at `0.2*atr_abs`. **Both arms pre-registered together (multiplicity 2, Bonferroni, alpha 0.025) BEFORE the detector existed** — the contract was sealed and floor-green (16/16) while `driver.py` did not yet exist. **ARM A (entry = displacement-bar close): n=944**, gross expectancy **+0.0080R**, t=+0.23, CI97.5% `[-0.0707, +0.0867]` — **crosses zero**; TP 478 / SL 398 / TIMEOUT 68, median risk **2.64 ATR** so TP-at-2-ATR implies a **0.76:1** payoff at a ~50.6% hit rate. **ARM B (entry = retest-bar close): n=462**, gross **-0.0835R**, t=-1.15, CI97.5% `[-0.2456, +0.0785]` — **crosses zero**; TP 123 / SL 328 / TIMEOUT 11, median risk **0.78 ATR** so a **2.55:1** payoff at a ~26.6% hit rate. The two arms are structurally OPPOSITE constructions — a near-coin-flip with sub-1R payoff, and a 2.5:1 payoff bought with a collapsed hit rate — and **both land at zero**, which is the signature of a surface with no exploitable directional asymmetry rather than of one mistuned parameter. **0 PROMOTE; neither arm selected** (the two-arm design did not become a hidden selection mechanism). Artifacts: `results/visual_crt/mc_vcrt_xauusd_m15_v1/{ledger_arm_A.jsonl, ledger_arm_B.jsonl, metrics.json}`, contract `configs/research/measurement_contracts/instances/MC-VCRT-XAUUSD-M15-V1.json`, floor `tests/research/test_visual_crt_trade_object.py` (32 tests).
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      —
- Owner:         claude (user-authorized 2026-08-17, CH-visual-crt-lane2-seal / P-VCRT-03)
- Note:          **DIAGNOSTIC ONLY — this is not an economic validation and not an economic refutation of tradability in general.** `economic_claims_allowed: false` on the sealed contract (`mt00`/`mt01` UNRUN; repo-wide 0/27 E-MT-01 probes; `MEASUREMENT_LAYER_STATUS = OPEN`). **The null does NOT depend on the cost model.** Net expectancy is decisively negative (A -0.556R, B -1.262R, both t < -13) but that is **cost-dominated by an uncalibrated constant**: `CM-XAUUSD-LEGACY-12BPS-UNCALIBRATED` costs **0.564R (A)** and **1.178R (B)** per trade, because 12bps of a ~3,300 gold price is ~4 price units against ATR-scaled stops — Arm B's tighter stop makes it far more cost-sensitive. Treating 12bps as a calibrated metals cost is a declared prohibited substitution (`FC-COST-MODEL-SUBSTITUTION`); the metals profile `MP-METALS-MT5` is still `DRAFT`. **[REFINED 2026-08-19 by F-082 — this finding's verdict is UNCHANGED]** the uncalibrated constant is now quantified against the repo's own MEASURED broker calibration: the flat 12bps charges **0.5394R** where the measured per-leg broker cost charges **0.0477R (SL exit) / 0.0354R (TP exit)** at `sl_atr_mult=1.0` and median ATR $7.342 — an over-charge of **11.3x / 15.2x**. `MP-METALS-MT5` has since been seeded with those measured components (still `DRAFT`, still not admissible). This REFINES the caveat and does not touch the verdict: the load-bearing gross results (+0.0080R / -0.0835R, both CI crossing zero) are cost-independent by construction, so **0 PROMOTE stands**. **[RE-MEASURED 2026-08-19 - F-084, verdict UNCHANGED]** the object was re-run under a new sealed instance `MC-VCRT-XAUUSD-M15-V2` (the path this contract's own kill_criteria mandates for a cost/exit-model change), with the measured broker cost (SEM-015) and honest stop fills (SEM-016). Both arms REJECT again: A gross -0.000396R net -0.0487R, B gross -0.1050R net -0.2107R. The null therefore survives the corrected measurement basis and is INFORMATION-driven, not a cost artifact. V1 was reproduced byte-identically before V2 was built; this row is unedited and V1 stays sealed. See also F-083: V1's declared OOS split and controls were never executed - which does not affect this REJECT, because controls are hurdles a POSITIVE must clear. The load-bearing claim is therefore the **gross** result, which is ~0 on both arms at n=944/462. **Arm B rests on a declared modelling substitution:** `impulse = |displacement.close - sweep.sweep_price|` stands in for the engine's `rng.size`, undefined for a level-valued pool — so Arm B's null bounds *this construction under that substitution* and says nothing about `crt_engine_v2.try_expansion_to_retest` (`FC-PIPELINE-SUBSTITUTION-LAUNDERED-AS-CANON`). **Scope:** one instrument (XAUUSD), one timeframe (M15), one pool vocabulary; session-derived pools were excluded from V1 by design because F-066 makes the MT5 session label wrong on 53.36% of bars. Per the sealed kill criteria, retuning any frozen dimension after seeing this result requires a **new `MC-*` id**, never an edit to V1. Extends the F-019…F-042 directional null ladder to a **pool-founded (chart-visible-liquidity) ontology**, the first founding structure genuinely distinct from the engine's own envelope. Grants **no G001, no promotion, no production authority**; `ACTIVE_VERSION` unchanged (`v2_htfcrt_2026_08`); CRT stays REOPENED; SEM-012 stays `CHARACTERIZED`. **CORRECTED 2026-08-26 (CH-measurement-provenance-boundary):** the `Contract:` field carried the bare sha256 `12be71beaf5098451d46d301eb39fed429633f39888d6da0dd05225fc7b8de54` — the content hash of the sealed instance, used because id-citation was unavailable before RC-1 (2026-08-19, `tests/test_current_findings.py`). It now names `MC-VCRT-XAUUSD-M15-V1` directly; the hash is preserved here and still resolves to that file byte-for-byte (§6.2 rule 4).
### F-082 · The XAUUSD research cost model was ~11x too punitive AND its stop fills were free — two opposite instrument defects, both now measured against the repo's own broker calibration and both corrected behind default-off config gates
- Type:          ARCHITECTURE
- Family:        RF-EXIT-COST-PATH
- Contract:      MP-METALS-MT5
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Certain
- Validated:     2026-08-19
- Revalidate-by: 2027-02-14
- Evidence:      Two independent defects in the measurement instrument, pulling in OPPOSITE directions. **(1) Cost over-charge.** `src/research/costs.py` applies a flat `DEFAULT_ROUND_TRIP_BPS = 12.0` to every instrument — a constant its own docstring scopes to "falsification, not execution realism" and admits "slightly OVER-penalizes". On XAUUSD at ~$3,300/oz that bills **$3.96/oz round-trip**. The repo's own MEASURED broker calibration — live MT5 terminal, ICMarketsSC-Demo, run 2026-08-06, `results/research/xauusd_mt5_cost_calibration/xauusd_mt5_cost_calibration_manifest_20260806T134255Z.json` sha256 `6ce4abf5…`, **all five component statuses MEASURED** — decomposes the real cost as half-spread **$0.045**, commission **$0.040/side**, STOP slippage median **$0.090** (n=7, p90 $0.320), swap long/short **−0.5601/+0.3834** per oz/night. Applied per-leg this is **$0.35 round-trip on a stop exit / $0.26 on a take-profit exit**. At `sl_atr_mult=1.0` and XAUUSD median ATR $7.342 the flat model charges **0.5394R** where the measured model charges **0.0477R (SL) / 0.0354R (TP)** — an over-charge of **11.3x / 15.2x**. **(2) Free stop fills.** `src/research/measurement/forward_walk.py` computed a stop exit as `rr = (trail_stop − entry)/risk_distance`, i.e. **exactly −1.000R regardless of how far the triggering bar gapped through the level**. Measured on the full 47,275-bar XAUUSD M15 corpus via a synthetic fixed-geometry probe grid (stride-97, 1.0/2.0 ATR, both directions, 970 signals → 649 SL_HIT): **649 of 649 stop exits returned exactly −1.000000R**. Charging the measured slippage plus true gap fills costs **−0.026R per stop** on average; gap-through is **rare but severe** — 3 of 649 (0.5%) triggering bars opened beyond the stop, gap excess median $2.00 / max $11.84 (≈3.4 ATR), worst single fill **−2.5345R**. Remediation is additive and default-inert: new `ComponentCostModel` (SEM-015) alongside an untouched `CostModel`; new `AdverseFill` + `adverse_fill` parameter (SEM-016) on `forward_walk`; `costs.cost_model` selector on `ResearchConfig` reusing the load-bearing `entry_ttl` conditional-`meaningful` precedent; `configs/research/measurement_contracts/metals_mt5.v1.json` seeded from the manifest (crypto/FX deliberately left `PENDING_CALIBRATION` — no measured data exists for them). **Parity proved:** all 23 parsable `configs/research/*.json` keep byte-identical `config_sha256`; the `forward_walk` default path is bit-identical over the corpus probe (sha256 `7019b32e…` before == after); `SCHEMA_HASH` `f52bf5d3…`, `CANONICAL_FEATURE_DIM` 48 and the active `config_hash` `7de09f62…` all unchanged. Floors: `tests/test_component_cost_model.py`, `tests/test_cost_model_parity.py`, `tests/test_forward_walk_adverse_fill.py`, `tests/test_measurement_profile_calibration.py` (76 tests). Manifest: `docs/governance/build_manifests/CH-cost-model-broker-truth.impact.json`.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — (reverses nothing; see Note)
- Owner:         claude (user-authorized 2026-08-19: "fix the ruler first" + "build it, measure nothing yet")
- Note:          **SCOPE IS XAUUSD / METALS ONLY.** This does NOT say the falsification ladder was measured with a broken ruler. For crypto majors (F-019…F-034) a 12bps round trip is approximately right for a taker-fee venue and no over-charge is claimed. For FX, F-035 already recorded the opposite problem (12bps ≈ 1.8–2.5x the median FX M15 bar) and that caveat stands unchanged. **This finding REFINES F-081, it does not reverse it.** F-081 had already quarantined the constant as `CM-XAUUSD-LEGACY-12BPS-UNCALIBRATED`, reported its 0.564R/1.178R per-trade cost, declared `FC-COST-MODEL-SUBSTITUTION` prohibited, and correctly made the **gross** result load-bearing. F-081's REJECT therefore stands entirely on its own terms: Arm A gross **+0.0080R** with CI crossing zero, Arm B gross **−0.0835R** with CI crossing zero. **Correcting the cost model cannot create an edge from a gross result that is already zero, and no such claim is made here.** **NO ECONOMIC AUTHORITY, NO RE-MEASUREMENT.** Nothing was re-run; no finding's verdict is recomputed; `MP-METALS-MT5` stays `DRAFT` and `economic_claims_allowed` stays false (`mt00`/`mt01` UNRUN, 0/27 E-MT-01 probes, `MEASUREMENT_LAYER_STATUS = OPEN`). Seeding a calibration is not admissibility (MEASUREMENT_CONTRACT.md §2 E4). **DECLARED WEAKNESSES:** stop slippage rests on **n=7** near-market DEMO stop fills and likely UNDERSTATES gap-driven barrier slip; MARKET-order slippage was `INSUFFICIENT_DATA` (n=0 fills) so entry slippage is a declared **conservative proxy** from the STOP median (`entry_slippage_basis=PROXY_FROM_STOP`), never a zero; the probe grid is synthetic fixed-geometry, so the −0.026R figure characterises the **instrument**, not the spine's real signal set. **TWO E-001 CORRECTIONS made before registration:** (a) an in-session estimate of ~53% gap-through was an inference bug in a scratch probe (it compared realised R against a slippage floor instead of testing `bar.open` against the stop) — direct measurement gives **0.5%**, recorded in SEM-016; (b) the source calibration's headline `c_per_side = $0.175` bundles stop slippage into **every** side because ZONE-X, which commissioned it, places stops on both legs — doubling it would double-charge a take-profit exit in this architecture, which enters on a market order, so costs are applied **per-leg**, not as `2 × c_per_side`. **ADJACENT DEFECTS REPORTED, NOT FIXED** (each needs separate authorization): `UltronRiskGate`'s spread+slippage RR tax (`src/core/ultron_risk_gate.py:229`) and FRAG-6 tight-stop guard (`:305`) are structurally DORMANT — `spread_pips`/`slippage_pips`/`min_sl_pips`/`pip_size` appeared in **zero** config files, so `min_rr_ratio=1.5` is enforced on raw cost-free reward:risk; they are now DECLARED at their current effective values (byte-identical, `config_hash` unchanged) purely to close the F-018/F-056 silent-default class. `pip_size`'s in-code default `0.0001` is the **FX-major** pip and is wrong for XAUUSD (0.01) and meaningless for crypto. `capital_management.pip_value_per_lot` omits **XAUUSD** entirely — the repo's only probe instrument. `execution_planner.default_account_balance = 10000.0` USD and `capital_management.total_capital_inr = 100000` (≈$1,190) are two unreconciled capital truths (§6.2 `TruthConflict`, no winner picked). Separately observed: the ACTIVE production config `configs/production/v2_htfcrt_2026_08.json` is **untracked in git** — the F-071 class recurring. Grants **no G001, no promotion, no production authority**; `ACTIVE_VERSION` unchanged (`v2_htfcrt_2026_08`); CRT stays REOPENED.
### F-083 · A sealed measurement contract declared an OOS split and two control benchmarks and its run executed neither — 1 of 8 declared evidence artifacts existed on disk; the gap is invisible to every governance floor because nothing checks a contract against its own outputs
- Type:          GOVERNANCE
- Family:        RF-EXIT-COST-PATH
- Contract:      MC-VCRT-XAUUSD-M15-V1
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Certain
- Validated:     2026-08-19
- Revalidate-by: 2027-02-14
- Evidence:      `MC-VCRT-XAUUSD-M15-V1.json` declares `splits.scheme = single_holdout_chronologic` with `embargo` 96 M15 bars, a purge of train units whose 40-bar exit horizon overlaps the test start, `seed` 20260817, and "exact dates locked in `split_manifest` at run time"; and a `metrics.success_gate` reading "…AND beats the pre-registered controls (`random_entry`, `long_only`)". **Neither was implemented.** `src/research/visual_crt/driver.py` contained **zero** occurrences of `control`, `random_entry`, `long_only`, `oos`, `holdout`, `embargo`, `purge` or `split` (grep, 2026-08-19); the V1 `LedgerRow` carries no split or control field; and `results/visual_crt/mc_vcrt_xauusd_m15_v1/summary.json` reports only per-arm `n`/win-rate/expectancy/outcomes. Checking the contract's own `evidence_artifacts` block against disk: **1 of 8 declared paths exists** (`metrics.json`). Missing: `population_fingerprint.json`, `cost_exit_fixture.json`, `split_manifest.json`. (`mt00_report_path`/`mt01_mutation_coverage_path` are also absent but are HONESTLY declared UNRUN in `trust_status`, so they are not part of this gap; `feature_parity_path`/`label_rederive_path`/`code_commit` are explicit nulls.) A third, independent instance of the same class: `driver.py:140-150` implemented its own `_net_r` with a hardcoded `ROUND_TRIP_BPS = 12.0` and never imported `research.costs`, so F-082's `cost_model` selector could not reach this measurement at all. REMEDIATED in the same turn: `src/research/visual_crt/controls.py` implements the declared split (with embargo + purge + `split_manifest.json`) and both controls; `driver.py` takes injected `cost_model`/`adverse_fill`/`contract_id` bindings; `src/research/visual_crt/measure.py` executes a contract end-to-end and writes every declared artifact; floor `tests/research/test_vcrt_v2_contract.py` (24 tests). Executed under MC-VCRT-XAUUSD-M15-V2 — see F-084.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — (reverses nothing; see Note)
- Owner:         claude (user-authorized 2026-08-19, CH-vcrt-remeasure-v2, full-scope option)
- Note:          **F-081's VERDICT IS UNAFFECTED and is not reopened.** Controls and an out-of-sample split are hurdles a POSITIVE result must clear; an arm rejected at the absolute-expectancy stage never reaches them, so their absence cannot have converted an edge into a null. The gap binds in exactly one direction — when something comes back positive — which is why it was closed before the F-084 re-measurement rather than after. **THE GENERALISABLE FAILURE MODE, which is the point of this finding:** a sealed contract is a promise about what WILL be measured, and this repository had no mechanical check that a run honoured it. `tests/test_measurement_contract.py` validates contract SHAPE (schema, enum membership, lifecycle gating, hash recomputation); nothing compared a contract's declarations against the artifacts its run actually produced, so "declared but never executed" was indistinguishable from "executed" to every floor. That is the same silent-gap class as F-079 (a skipped measurement being indistinguishable from an absent one) and the same class as F-056 (`partial_tp_fraction` strict-read then DISCARDED) — presence of a declaration is not evidence of execution. **RECOMMENDED, NOT DONE** (needs its own authorized turn): a generic floor asserting that for every sealed `MC-*` instance whose run directory exists, each non-null `evidence_artifacts` path resolves, or the instance carries an explicit UNRUN declaration for it — the same fail-closed shape F-079 applied. Not written here because it would fail immediately on other sealed instances that this turn did not measure and has no authority to adjudicate. Scope: this finding is about the V1 instance and the mechanism; it makes **no** claim that other `MC-*` instances are complete or incomplete. Grants **no** economic, promotion or G001 authority.

### F-084 · The Visual CRT null SURVIVES the corrected measurement basis — under measured broker costs and honest stop fills both pre-registered arms still REJECT, confirming a pre-registered prediction; and the first-ever control run shows Arm A failing to beat passive long exposure
- Type:          ECONOMIC
- Family:        RF-EXIT-COST-PATH
- Contract:      MC-VCRT-XAUUSD-M15-V2
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Likely
- Validated:     2026-08-19
- Revalidate-by: 2027-02-14
- Evidence:      Re-measurement of SEM-012 v2 under a NEW sealed instance (`MC-VCRT-XAUUSD-M15-V2`), which V1's own `kill_criteria` mandates for any change to the cost or exit model. **EXACTLY TWO surfaces differ from V1** — `costs` (`CM-XAUUSD-LEGACY-12BPS-UNCALIBRATED` → `CM-XAUUSD-COMPONENT-MEASURED-V1`, SEM-015, manifest sha256 `6ce4abf5…`) and `exits` (`EX-VCRT-INTRABAR-V1` → `EX-VCRT-INTRABAR-ADVERSE-V2`, SEM-016 `AdverseFill(stop_slippage=0.09, model_gaps=True)`); `population` (same corpus, same sha256 `4d73f5ce…`, 47,275 bars), `features`, `labels`, `pipeline_identity`, `prohibited_substitutions`, `kill_criteria`, all geometry, horizon and both arm definitions are byte-identical, mechanically asserted by `tests/research/test_vcrt_v2_contract.py`. **GATE: V1 was reproduced byte-identically first** — regenerated ledgers matched the committed `ledger_arm_{A,B}.jsonl` exactly, and V1 re-run through the new pipeline returned its published figures to 6 decimals. **RESULT — 0 PROMOTE, both arms REJECT.** **ARM A** n=944: gross **−0.000396R** (t=−0.011, CI97.5 `[−0.0797, +0.0789]`, crosses zero), net **−0.048701R** (CI `[−0.1286, +0.0312]`, crosses zero), PF 0.899. **ARM B** n=462: gross **−0.104971R** (t=−1.439, CI `[−0.2683, +0.0584]`, crosses zero), net **−0.210696R** (CI `[−0.3754, −0.0460]`), PF 0.742. Cost fell as predicted (A net −0.5559R → −0.0487R; B −1.2617R → −0.2107R) while the adverse fill pushed GROSS the other way (A +0.0080R → −0.0004R; B −0.0835R → −0.1050R) — the two corrections partially offset, which is why they must never be described as one "costs were too high" fix. **PRE-REGISTERED PREDICTION CONFIRMED** (sealed in `CH-vcrt-remeasure-v2.impact.json` before the controls code was written): predicted A net −0.03…−0.08 (actual −0.0487 ✓), B gross −0.09…−0.12 (actual −0.1050 ✓), B net −0.15…−0.30 (actual −0.2107 ✓); A gross predicted 0.000…+0.007, actual −0.000396, marginally below the stated floor and still indistinguishable from zero. **FILL MECHANICS VERIFIED:** V1 booked **398/398 and 328/328 stop exits at exactly −1.000000R**; under V2 every stop costs more than 1R (worst −1.3425R; 25 of 398 and 42 of 328 beyond −1.05R), and entry/outcome/duration sets are IDENTICAL between runs, proving the fill model changed only the exit PRICE and never which bar triggered. **CONTROLS AND OOS SPLIT — EXECUTED FOR THE FIRST TIME** (F-083): Arm A `long_only` net **+0.033082R** vs Arm A's **−0.048701R**, so the arm does NOT beat passive long exposure at its own entry bars; Arm A beats `random_entry` (−0.091257R). Arm B loses to `long_only` (−0.194338R) but its single declared draw of `random_entry` (−0.368221R) sits below it — **and that single draw is misleading**, see the seed-stability result in the Note. OOS: A IS n=754 −0.053766R / OOS n=189 −0.028484R; B IS n=369 −0.196759R / OOS n=92 −0.256501R — both negative in both partitions. Artifacts: `results/visual_crt/mc_vcrt_xauusd_m15_v2/` (ledgers, 4 control ledgers, `split_manifest.json`, `population_fingerprint.json`, `cost_exit_fixture.json`, `metrics.json`, `summary.json`); runner `scripts/research/vcrt_remeasure_v2.py`.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — (confirms F-081 under a corrected basis; reverses nothing)
- Owner:         claude (user-authorized 2026-08-19: agreed UNK-COST-02, authorized a Visual CRT re-measurement at full scope)
- Note:          **THE LOAD-BEARING CLAIM: the Visual CRT null is INFORMATION-driven, not a cost artifact.** F-081 could only rest on gross because its net was cost-dominated; V2 shows the null survives when the cost model is the measured broker one and the fill model is honest. This is the question F-082 was built to make answerable, and the answer is that correcting the ruler does not rescue this object. **NO ECONOMIC CLAIM.** `economic_claims_allowed` is false on V2 as on V1 (`mt00`/`mt01` UNRUN, 0/27 E-MT-01 probes, `MEASUREMENT_LAYER_STATUS = OPEN`); a calibrated cost model does not confer admissibility (MEASUREMENT_CONTRACT.md §2 E4). **V2 IS A REPLICATION, NOT AN INDEPENDENT TEST** — same corpus, same two pre-registered arms, inheriting V1's Bonferroni α=0.025 and spending no new alpha (declared as UNK-VCRT-02 BEFORE the run). Had either arm turned positive it would NOT have been registrable as an edge; it would have required a fresh pre-registration on unseen data. **CONTROL RESULTS ARE POINT ESTIMATES, NOT SIGNIFICANCE TESTS** — no paired difference test was pre-registered or run, and the contract's success gate says "beats the controls" without naming a test. **THE SINGLE-DRAW `random_entry` CONTROL IS TOO NOISY TO GATE ON AT THIS n, AND ITS DECLARED DRAW IS MISLEADING FOR ARM B.** The contract seeds one draw (`splits.seed` 20260817). Repeating the draw over 100 consecutive seeds (descriptive diagnostic, not the gate): Arm A's net −0.048701R beats the control in **68/100** draws (control mean −0.064014R, sd 0.033248, p10 −0.106509 / p90 −0.017349); Arm B's net −0.210696R beats it in only **22/100** draws (control mean −0.145523R, sd 0.088506, p10 −0.244448 / p90 −0.023584). So the declared draw's verdict that Arm B "beats random_entry" is an artifact of one unusually adverse control sample — the honest reading is that **Arm B is WORSE than random entry in 78% of draws**, which strengthens rather than weakens its REJECT. The control's own sampling sd (0.0885) is comparable to the quantity being compared, so a one-draw random control is not a sound gate at n=462; a distribution over draws is. Recorded as a limitation of the V1/V2 control SPECIFICATION (inherited verbatim, not introduced here); changing it requires a V3. An earlier in-session run of this same control under seed 20260819 gave −0.059720R (A) and −0.185989R (B), flipping Arm B's comparison — the discrepancy that prompted the seed-stability check. The declared seed 20260817 governs; no seed was selected on results. Specifically: `long_only`'s +0.033082R has CI97.5 `[−0.0505, +0.1167]` and **crosses zero**, so it is NOT evidence that passive long exposure is profitable — the honest statement is that Arm A's point estimate sits below a control that is itself indistinguishable from zero. Arm B and both of its controls are net-negative with CIs excluding zero. **The long_only comparison is nonetheless the more informative of the two controls on this corpus**, because XAUUSD trended up across 2024-05 → 2026-05 and a directional object must beat being long at the same moments, not merely beat zero. **CARRIED WEAKNESSES:** stop slippage rests on n=7 near-market DEMO fills and likely understates gap-driven slip (p90 $0.32 vs $0.09 median); entry slippage is a declared conservative PROXY from the STOP median (`PROXY_FROM_STOP`), not a measurement, because MARKET slippage was INSUFFICIENT_DATA (n=0 fills); Arm B still rests on V1's declared `impulse`-for-`rng.size` substitution and says nothing about `crt_engine_v2.try_expansion_to_retest`. **Retuning any frozen dimension after seeing these outcomes requires a V3, never an edit to V2.** Extends the F-019…F-042 / F-081 directional null ladder. Grants **no G001, no promotion, no production authority**; `ACTIVE_VERSION` unchanged (`v2_htfcrt_2026_08`); CRT stays REOPENED; SEM-012 stays `CHARACTERIZED`.

### F-085 · The FeatureStore canonical ingestion boundary never ran on the live path — a missing `global` declaration discarded the singleton, so every live bar was scored on un-validated features and died inside ZoneGate; three further contract breaks were hidden behind it
- Type:          ARCHITECTURE
- Family:        RF-FEATURE-ONTOLOGY
- Contract:      UNKNOWN
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Certain
- Validated:     2026-08-19
- Revalidate-by: 2027-02-14
- Evidence:      `live_engine_hook._load_engine_config` assigns `_feature_store = FeatureStore(...)` (`:394`) but its `global` statement (`:279`) listed only `_ENGINE_CONFIG_CACHE, _feature_monitor`. The assignment therefore bound a function-local and was discarded; the module singleton (`:92`) stayed `None`, so the guard `if _STORE_AVAILABLE and _feature_store is not None` never fired and `engine_input` remained the reduced `_build_engine_input()` dict. Symptom observed on the 2026-08-19 17:27 paper run: `closed_bars=80 process_calls=0` with two `FEATURE_REJECT` records reading `Missing canonical keys: [...29 names...]`, raised by `filter_canonical_inputs` (`zone_gate_engine.py:164-166`) — ZoneGate received 19 of the 48 canonical keys. **Three further breaks were masked by it** (none discoverable while the boundary never executed): (1) `_build_ohlcv_and_auxiliary` is a hardcoded dict literal whose docstring already promised "all remaining CANONICAL_FEATURES", but it predated schema v5.0 and omitted all 12 SMC/liquidity keys (F-076); (2) it also passed three NON-canonical keys (`wick_size`, `body_to_total_wick_ratio`, `body_to_range_ratio`, GD-001/GD-002 dual-emissions) which `FeatureStore._validate_schema` rejects as extras — they never survived this boundary anyway, since the store emits canonical-only; (3) `_normalize_session` carried a hardcoded 4-value map while `features/session_classifier.SessionOrdinal` (the declared single owner) defines FIVE, so every `CLOSED=4` bar raised — 24 of 122 (20%) on a real 2024 XAUUSD sample. All four fixed. Certified by deterministic historical replay of `data/mt5/XAUUSD_M15.csv`: Arm A (bar injection) 200 bars → 122 attempts / **122 process calls** / 122 terminal `NO_ORDER` records / 0 errors; Arm B (tick replay through `BarBuilder`, round-trip-asserted) 199 bars → 121/121. Two identical runs are byte-identical once wall-clock `ts` is excluded. Floor: `tests/test_live_rail_replay_cert.py` (12 tests).
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — (no prior conclusion claimed this path worked; F-073 already recorded that no production caller exists)
- Owner:         claude (user-approved certification plan 2026-08-19)
- Note:          **THE GENERALISABLE FAILURE MODE:** the T-11 hardening of 2026-07-19 made this boundary fail CLOSED when `FeatureStore.process` RAISES, and its comment states "a validation failure must REJECT the tick, never downgrade it." That hardening was unreachable — the block it protects was never entered, because the store was never constructed. **Skipped validation was indistinguishable from absent validation**, the same silent-gap class as F-079 (a skipped measurement vs an absent one), F-056 (`partial_tp_fraction` strict-read then DISCARDED) and F-083 (a contract declared but never executed). A guard is not enforcement until something proves it runs. The fix adds that proof: the guard now calls the cache-idempotent builder and RAISES if the singleton is still `None`, so the skip cannot silently return. **A PRE-REGISTERED PREDICTION FAILED and is recorded as such:** the sealed impact manifest predicted that fixing the `global` alone would carry a bar to a decision, on the strength of the auxiliary docstring's "all remaining CANONICAL_FEATURES". It did not — two further seams (the 12 missing v5 keys and the 3 non-canonical extras) had to be closed, because the docstring described intent while the literal had drifted. **SCOPE (no overclaim):** F-073 is UNCHANGED — there is still no production live loop, so this defect had **no production consequence** and no production-loss claim is made; backtests use `backtest_v2`'s own path and never read this singleton (grep-verified: no module outside `live_engine_hook.py` references `_feature_store`). This is an INTEGRATION certification — it proves a historical bar can travel raw OHLCV → canonical features → FeatureStore → `HookedLiveEngine.process` → a recorded decision, deterministically, with zero orders. It makes **NO** claim that those decisions are correct or profitable: all 122 were `NO_ORDER`, dominated by `invalid_session:asia` rejects at the adapter stage. F-010 stays OPEN (no exit loop → live PnL still unverified). Grants **no G001, no promotion, no live authority**; `ACTIVE_VERSION` unchanged (`v2_htfcrt_2026_08`), `dry_run` still true.

### F-086 · The declared feature and state vocabulary does not mark a profitable entry bar on XAUUSD M15 — outcome-first inversion finds real conditional information (6.2–7.6× a measured permutation null) and zero economic consumability (0 of 84 state cells clear zero on any of 8 arms)
- Type:          ECONOMIC
- Family:        RF-CRT-STRUCTURE
- Contract:      UNKNOWN
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Likely
- Validated:     2026-08-21
- Revalidate-by: 2027-02-14
- Evidence:      **Inverts the repo's entry-first habit** — every prior program proposed a detection rule and measured what it caught, coupling statistical power to detector throughput (Program 2 got n=47, F-081 Arm B n=462, the spine n=13). This simulates a trade at EVERY bar in BOTH directions under the real Entry/SL/TP1/TP2 geometry, keeps the winners, and only then asks what the declared vocabulary was saying. Scope is exactly `docs/analysis/feature-identity-state-inventory-2026-08-19.xlsx` (48 decision-vector slots, 19 ontology feature-state families, CRT resolver states, parent/HTF machine states) — nothing outside it, nothing inside it skipped, including surfaces prior findings called inert. **Stage 0**: `scripts/research/build_bar_matrix.py` → 47,197 rows × 124 cols (no such joined artifact existed; each layer previously lived in a different script at a different cadence). **Stage 0.5 — a blocking instrument gate, because a null from a broken detector is indistinguishable from a null from an efficient market**: `scripts/research/validate_oracle_harness.py`, 5 checks, PASS — planted signal at AUC 0.60/0.55/0.53 recovered at 0.606/0.549/0.532; a full-horizon label shift collapses 0.596 → 0.499/0.502; per-bar identities hold to max error 2.8e-14 and break (error 176/162) under a 1-bar shift; a shuffled label returns the base rate exactly (−0.26914 vs −0.26914). **Stage 1**: 377,256 labelled units (47,157 bars × 2 directions × 2 stop geometries × 2 tie-breaks) in 10.6s via `src/research/oracle/multi_tp_walk.py`, `src/research/oracle/reference_walker.py`, `src/research/oracle/labeler.py`. **Stage 3** (`scripts/research/oracle_pattern_scan.py`, TRAIN only): **L1** max |AUC−0.5| anywhere = 0.048, on `momentum_score` — itself flagged contaminated (F-061/F-064 FM-023 dimensional mix saturates it on 99.7% of XAUUSD bars, so it is near-constant for a *units* reason); every other feature 0.51–0.53 and every top-decile expectancy negative on every arm. **L2** 0 of 84 state cells clear zero on the net basis across all 8 arm×direction combinations; cells do shift *relative* expectancy (best on the primary arm `candle_direction=BULL_STRONG` at −0.102R against a −0.294R base). **L3** 68,473 depth-≤3 conjunctions per direction, pruned by *effective* support (blocks, not rows), null measured by block-permutation rather than assumed: long 12,898 beat base vs permuted 1,592/1,942/1,549 (**×7.6**), short 13,068 vs 2,099/1,477/2,729 (**×6.2**); but clearing zero is 6 vs permuted 1/3/0 (long) and 5 vs 0/0/0 (short) — inside the noise. Overlap is measured, not assumed: label autocorrelation +0.292 at lag 1 → +0.164 at lag 2 → +0.003 by lag 20, effective independent sample **941 per direction, not 47,157**, so every interval is a block bootstrap sized to the horizon (floor `tests/research/test_oracle_labeler.py`, 13 tests, incl. `test_block_bootstrap_is_wider_than_iid_on_autocorrelated_data`). Artifacts: `results/research/oracle_scan/XAUUSD_M15/scan_report_train.json`, `l2_states_{long,short}_train.csv`, `l1_marginal_{long,short}_train.csv`, `l3_conjunctions_{long,short}_train.csv`; labels `results/research/oracle_labels/XAUUSD_M15/labels.csv` (81 MB, sha256 `3027ea97…`). Write-up: `docs/analysis/profitable-entry-oracle-2026-08-21.md`.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — (does not confirm or overturn any prior finding; different measurement basis, independent evidence by construction. **Contamination posture was explicit**: prior hypothesis results were treated as *unverified*, not as evidence — no prior finding narrowed the search, no prior artifact was reused as input (every corpus rebuilt at schema v5.0 from raw OHLCV), and the study cites no F-id as support for its own conclusion. A null here does not confirm the prior nulls F-019…F-035; a positive would not have overturned them.)
- Owner:         claude (registered 2026-08-21 under a user-approved docs/research-only plan)
- Note:          **INFORMATION EXISTS / VALUE DOES NOT** — Authority-Ladder rung 1 (§6.5), granting nothing above it: no fusion weight, no sizing, no production authority, no G001. The two halves are separately established and disagree usefully: a 6–8× excess over a *measured* null with tight permutation draws is not a multiple-comparisons artifact (the vocabulary genuinely carries conditional information about *relative* outcome), while 0/84 clearing zero says none of it is economically consumable. **THE DISTINCTION THAT DOES ALL THE WORK:** the base rate is ≈ −0.29R, so *beating the base rate* means losing less than average, NOT making money. Conflating the two would upgrade a real measurement into a false economic claim (E-001). **The gross-basis exception is drift, not information** — the two arms showing many cells clearing zero (65 and 56) are exactly the arms whose base is already positive (+0.048, +0.041), because XAUUSD rose across 2024-05 → 2026-05; controlled against passive same-direction exposure they collapse 65→8, 56→1, 2→0, which is why `long_only` is the binding control and not zero. **SELECTING BARS BY REALISED OUTCOME IS LOOKAHEAD BY CONSTRUCTION** — Stages 0–3 describe the past; they are not an edge and cannot become one without forward validation. **The 236-bar non-overlapping holdout is UNSPENT**, and was honestly quantified *before* mining rather than discovered afterwards; it is thin for validating an n≈60 conjunction, so widening it or adding an instrument is a decision surfaced, not taken. **`crt_state_resolved` is the RESOLVER, not the engine** — this run's dwell (RANGE 21,745 / SWEEP 15,186 / EXPANSION 7,092) differs substantially from the engine reference pinned in `market_crt_states.yaml` (RANGE 35,159 / SWEEP 6,995 / EXPANSION 4,605), consistent with F-069's structurally-config-unreachable determination; any pattern keyed on a resolved state is a statement about the resolver until an engine re-measurement runs. **Only 3 permutations** — enough for the 6–8× base-rate excess (order-of-magnitude gap, tight draws) but too coarse to put a p-value on a beats-zero count of 5 or 6; the short side's 5-against-0/0/0 is suggestive and deliberately NOT claimed. Two ontology nodes registered BEFORE the code that implements them (§6.6) and mutation-tested: **SEM-017** `MULTI_TARGET_PARTIAL_EXIT`, **SEM-018** `PROFITABLE_ENTRY_ORACLE_LABEL`. `economic_claims_allowed: false`; mt00/mt01 UNRUN, so `Contract: UNKNOWN` is honest rather than omitted. Scope: XAUUSD M15, one broker calibration, one corpus — it does NOT say no edge exists on this instrument, only that *this vocabulary* under *this geometry* over *this corpus* does not carry one. Authority: research only. **LOCKED 2026-08-23 (`CH-identity-l5-executed-record-freeze`):** this finding remains the definitive answer on M15 structural tradeability. The certified 4-of-4 L5 rows in [`governance/xauusd_identity_certification_2y.md`](governance/xauusd_identity_certification_2y.md) are a different object — CRTEngine executed closes under `backtest_ledger`/`none_gross`/`engine_intrabar` — and do **not** reopen this inversion. No 100% window scan. No TradeLib.

### F-087 · The exit is not the binding constraint — gross expectancy is ≈0 at the every-bar level, net is just −cost, cost is set by stop WIDTH not stop POLICY, the whole 12-policy dynamic-stop axis sits below the noise floor, and 0 of 16 paired tests find any interaction with the entry information
- Type:          ECONOMIC
- Family:        RF-EXIT-COST-PATH
- Contract:      UNKNOWN
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Likely
- Validated:     2026-08-21
- Revalidate-by: 2027-02-14
- Evidence:      Answers the one question F-086 left open — *is the information failing to cross zero because of the EXIT, because of COST, or because the information is simply TOO SMALL?* **Stage A (ceiling before search, 35s, computed before any policy existed** — if a perfect all-knowing exit could not profit, no rule could and the search is pointless): median capture ratio (`capture_ratio_p50`) is NEGATIVE on **9 of 12** cells — the exit turns a trade that WAS in profit into a loss on a majority of cells; the 3 positives are all `fixed_atr|long`, i.e. the drift side [**CORRECTED 2026-08-21: 10 of 12 -> 9 of 12.** Recounted directly from `results/research/exit_geometry/XAUUSD_M15/ceilings_cells.csv`: 9 negative, 3 positive. No other capture-like column in `ceilings_report.json` yields 10 either (`capture_ratio_p90` 0/12 negative, `mfe_capture` 0/12, `ceiling_utilization` 12/12), so the original figure was a miscount, not a different metric. The miscount originates in the write-up and propagated into this block on registration; conclusion unaffected — a majority still converts profit into loss]; on `fixed_atr/long/h96` holding passively returns **+1.368R** where the actual exit machinery returns **+0.007R**. **Stage B/C — 390 cells** (5 stop widths × 13 arms = production + 12 policies × 3 horizons × 2 directions, every cell over all 47,157 bars, entries held identical everywhere so any difference is attributable to the exit alone). *The decomposition*, mean over horizons and directions, by stop width: `disp_bar` (production) gross −0.1097 / cost 0.1707 / net −0.2804 · `fixed_0.5` −0.1119 / 0.1755 / −0.2874 · `fixed_1` −0.0332 / 0.0940 / −0.1272 · `fixed_2` −0.0099 / 0.0510 / −0.0609 · `fixed_3` **gross +0.0010** / 0.0349 / net −0.0339. Gross converges on zero as the stop widens and net is then just −cost; tight stops are gross-*negative* (−0.11R) because a near stop is reached by noise AND simultaneously pay ~5× the cost in R, since `cost_r = cost_price / risk_distance` — both terms penalise the same thing. *Cost is ~12× the entire dynamic-stop axis at every horizon independently*: h20 policy spread 0.0198R (0.49 CI) vs width spread 0.2540R (6.35 CI) = **12.8×**; h40 0.0212R (0.41 CI) vs 0.2521R (4.93 CI) = **11.9×**; h96 0.0205R (0.33 CI) vs 0.2545R (4.14 CI) = **12.4×**. *2 of 390 cells clear zero and both are drift* — `fixed_3/long/h96` arms `fixed` (+0.0582R, CI [+0.0009, +0.1206]) and `breakeven_at_1.5r` (+0.0560R, CI [+0.0001, +0.1168]): widest stop, longest horizon, long side of a rising instrument, both CI lower bounds within 0.001R of zero; against passive same-direction exposure over the same 96 bars (**+0.2290R**) they capture only 25% and 24%, so the pre-declared rule that an unconditionally positive cell is a defect signal before it is a result resolves them as drift, badly captured. *The production half-way trail, audited* (a question never previously asked): on `disp_bar` — the geometry production actually runs — h20/long −0.26948 vs `fixed` −0.25704 (Δ −0.01244), h20/short −0.31850 vs −0.30819 (Δ −0.01032), h40/long −0.26914 vs −0.25663 (Δ −0.01251), h40/short −0.31960 vs −0.31027 (Δ −0.00933) — rank **12th of 12 in all four combinations**, and 12/12 again on `fixed_0.5`, i.e. **8 of 8 tight-stop combinations, worst arm every time**, delta stable to three decimals. *Same-bar ambiguity*, on the 12 arms the restriction actually governs: mean |band| **0.261R** (range 0.200–0.308R) against policy spreads of 0.0198/0.0212/0.0205R = **12×–15×** (12.3× mean at h40 to 15.5× worst-arm at h20) [**SHARPENED 2026-08-21: 0.196R / 9.2×–14.5× -> 0.261R / 12×–15×.** The superseded 0.196R was a correctly-computed mean over ALL 16 rows of `same_bar_band.csv`, but 4 of those are `time_decay` arms whose band is ~0.0005R precisely BECAUSE they never read the running extreme — they are the mechanical control, not subjects of the restriction. Averaging them in diluted the figure and UNDERSTATED the constraint on the 12 arms it binds. Not an error in the original computation; a scope refinement. Restricted correctly, the restriction is stronger, so nothing downstream loosens] — measured on the real corpus, 2–3× the synthetic estimate it superseded, because real bars carry far larger intrabar ranges relative to close-to-close moves than Gaussian paths. **Stage D — the decisive test**: since every exit was measured on the same bars, the question collapses to whether market state predicts the per-bar DIFFERENCE between two exits. **0 of 16 comparisons show interaction** (10 clean level shifts, 6 indeterminate); smallest p anywhere **0.10** against an attainable floor of 0.05 given 19 permutations. Code: `src/research/oracle/exit_analysis.py`, `src/research/oracle/stop_policy.py`, `src/research/oracle/exit_sweep.py`; driver `scripts/research/exit_geometry_scan.py`. Floors: `tests/research/test_stop_policy.py` (28), `tests/research/test_exit_ceilings.py` (19). Artifacts: `results/research/exit_geometry/XAUUSD_M15/ceilings_report.json`, `sweep_cells.csv`, `same_bar_band.csv`, `interaction_report.json`. Write-up: `docs/analysis/exit-geometry-decomposition-2026-08-21.md`.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — **Does NOT confirm or overturn F-025.** Different exit object (two-target partial vs single-TP), different cost basis (SEM-015 measured components vs flat 12bps), different instrument (XAUUSD vs crypto majors). Arriving at a structurally similar conclusion from an independent basis is corroboration worth noting and is deliberately **not** cited as support in either direction.
- Owner:         claude (registered 2026-08-21 under a user-approved docs/research-only plan)
- Note:          **THE FOUR-LINE ANSWER:** (1) gross expectancy is ≈0 at the every-bar level, and an exit cannot create expectancy the entry did not contain — it can only decide how much of a zero-mean quantity gets converted into cost; (2) net = gross − cost and cost dominates, moved 4–6 CI widths by stop WIDTH almost entirely through `cost_r`; (3) the dynamic-stop axis is inert AND most of it is unmeasurable anyway; (4) no interaction with the entry information. So of exit / cost / information-size it is **not the exit** — and between the other two it is both, which are the same statement: *the information is not large enough to clear the cost that any real stop placement incurs.* **A RANKING RESTRICTION, APPLIED NOT NOTED:** every arm that reads the RUNNING EXTREME (all `ratchet_*`, all `breakeven_*`) carries a same-bar ambiguity band 9–15× the spread it would be used to measure, because a 15-minute candle cannot say whether the high came before or after the low — those arms are **NOT RANKABLE from M15 OHLC** and their ordering is explicitly not a finding; only level- or elapsed-time-triggered arms (`fixed`, `production`, `time_decay_*`) are licensed, and `time_decay_6_12`'s band of **+0.0003R** mechanically confirms the cause. This does not rescue anything: the causal arm is the *optimistic* end in 14 of 16 cases, so true values sit at or below what was measured. **THIS DOES NOT SAY THE PRODUCTION TRAIL SHOULD BE REMOVED.** The sign and rank are consistent across 8 independent geometry×direction×horizon combinations, but the magnitude (≈ −0.012R) is roughly **15–25% of ONE confidence-interval width** and those combinations share the same bars, so no p-value is computed from their agreement. The defensible statement is *the worst point estimate in the grid, consistently, on a grid entirely below the noise floor* — removal is a behaviour change requiring its own authorisation and a measured ΔG001 (§6.5). **No config was touched; `ACTIVE_VERSION` unchanged.** **THE UNCOMFORTABLE OBSERVATION, scoped:** 0 of 156 long configurations beat passive same-direction exposure, and since the widest stop both minimises cost and has gross ≈0, widening the stop converges toward holding — whose limit on this corpus is gold's own drift (+0.23R over 96 bars where the best exit configuration returns +0.058R). 103 of 156 short cells DO beat passive, but the best short cell in absolute terms is −0.065R, and beating a negative benchmark by losing less is not an edge. This is a measurement **about this corpus, not a recommendation**: passive exposure has an entirely different risk profile — no stop means unbounded drawdown, which the R-normalised comparison deliberately does not price. What it establishes is that the exit machinery on this instrument over this period subtracted from a passive baseline, which is a fact about where engineering effort has been going. **AN E-001 CORRECTION IS EMBEDDED:** a docstring and ontology note claiming that letting a stop update from the current bar's own high manufactures free money at scale was measurably WRONG — arming a stop sooner makes it *tighter* sooner, so the spike that arms a ratchet becomes the bar that exits on it (on 4,000 synthetic paths `ratchet_0.5r` was worse on 920 and better on 248; `breakeven_at_1r` was NEVER better). Fixed in three source files and the ontology, and pinned by a test asserting the *direction* so it cannot silently revert. Two ontology nodes registered BEFORE the code (§6.6), mutation-tested 6/6: **SEM-019** `DYNAMIC_STOP_POLICY`, **SEM-020** `EXIT_CAPTURE_DECOMPOSITION`. `economic_claims_allowed: false`; mt00/mt01 UNRUN. Scope: XAUUSD M15 under one broker calibration and one corpus. Authority: research only.

### F-088 · Every outcome-bearing result in this repository's history was measured on a different trade object than the one production actually trades — `forward_walk` models one target with no partial and no trail, while production realises 50% at TP1, moves the stop to a HALF-WAY point (not breakeven, despite the config key name) and runs the remainder to TP2
- Type:          ARCHITECTURE
- Family:        RF-LABEL-TRUTH
- Contract:      UNKNOWN
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Certain
- Validated:     2026-08-21
- Revalidate-by: 2027-02-14
- Evidence:      Production's exit object is a five-step sequence, source-verified not assumed: enter at the close of a specific candle → place the stop just beyond that candle's low (long) plus a buffer → set TP1 and TP2 as multiples of the risk distance → **on the TP1 transition close 50% and reassign `trade.sl_price` to `entry + 0.5*(tp1 − entry)`** (`ExecutionEngine.update_trade`, `src/config_layer/crt_engine_v2.py`) → let the remaining 50% run to TP2. The research kernel `src/research/measurement/forward_walk.py` expresses none of that: one TP, no partial, no trail. The blend is load-bearing in the backtest too — `src/runtime/backtest_v2.py` carries three exit-price blend sites keyed on `partial_tp_fraction` (F-056 recorded that the value was strict-read then DISCARDED at a hardcoded 0.5/0.5, including a `:2426` site an audit missed). **The naming trap**: the config key is `partial_tp_breakeven_enabled`, read strictly at `src/runtime/backtest_v2.py:2094` — it reads as move-the-stop-to-breakeven, but the code moves it to the half-way point, which is *already in profit*. The key name and the behaviour disagree and the code is what runs. **The gap is closed by a new kernel**, `src/research/oracle/multi_tp_walk.py`, certified three ways: floor `tests/research/test_multi_tp_walk_parity.py` (18 tests), an independent naive twin agreeing on 20,000 synthetic paths, and a real ledger fixture reproduced to 1e-10 (entry 2318.21 → tp1 2319.3401428571). Registered as ontology node **SEM-017** `MULTI_TARGET_PARTIAL_EXIT` (§6.6, registered before the implementing code, mutation-tested). Additive-safe: when `multi_tp_walk` later gained `stop_policy` and `trail_fraction=None` for F-087, all 377,256 on-disk oracle labels regenerated **byte-identically** (sha256 `3027ea97…` before and after). **A second source correction landed in the same object**: the effective same-bar tie-break is SL-FIRST, not the `TP2 > SL > TP1` branch order declared at `crt_engine_v2.py:2456` — `_intrabar_trigger_price` (`:2667`) collapses each bar to one scalar and tests `sl_touch` before any target (inline comment *"conservative: SL before TP1 on a spanning bar"*), so the backtest path was **already conservative**; the optimistic target-first precedence exists only in `analytics/sl_tp_comparator.simulate_exit`, which the backtest never calls. Measured divergence between the two conventions: **6.81%** of 188,628 paired units, worth +0.0866R across all units and +1.27R across those that differ — *smaller* than the SEM-015 measured cost (0.084–0.176R), so cost remains the dominant measurement-basis uncertainty on every arm. Write-ups: `docs/analysis/profitable-entry-oracle-2026-08-21.md` (the two build-time corrections), `docs/analysis/exit-geometry-decomposition-2026-08-21.md`.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — **This is an object/measurement-basis correction, NOT a reversal of any economic conclusion.** It does not re-open or re-grade F-019…F-035, F-081 or F-084: those remain valid statements about the object they measured. It records that the object was not production's, which bounds what a `forward_walk`-derived number may be said to describe. Whether any specific prior null would move under `multi_tp_walk` is UNMEASURED and is not claimed in either direction.
- Owner:         claude (registered 2026-08-21 under a user-approved docs/research-only plan)
- Note:          **SAME SILENT-GAP CLASS AS F-056 / F-079 / F-083 / F-085** — a declared thing that was never what actually ran, where the skipped case is indistinguishable from the absent one. Here the specific shape is: a research kernel whose *name* implies it walks the trade forward, silently walking a **simpler** trade than the engine executes, with nothing anywhere comparing the two. F-056's lesson generalises exactly — *presence + strict-read ≠ governed until the value reaches behaviour* — and this extends it one level up: *a measurement kernel is not a measurement of production until something proves it models the same object.* The three-way certification (property floor + independent twin + real ledger row to 1e-10) is that proof for `multi_tp_walk`. **THE NAMING TRAP IS RETAINED, NOT FIXED** — renaming `partial_tp_breakeven_enabled` would touch 10 files under `configs/production/` plus the strict read site, i.e. a config change requiring its own authorisation; it is instead documented at the authoritative surfaces (`docs/reference/config-reference.md` under `execution_planner`, `docs/topics/execution-planning.md`) and pinned by a mechanical guard, so a future session cannot resolve the divergence in the WRONG direction (changing behaviour to match the name) unnoticed. Same treatment F-048 gave `decision_engine.rr_threshold` (retained-but-RETIRED, hash-neutral). **SCOPE (no overclaim):** this is an ARCHITECTURE/measurement-basis finding — it makes **no** economic claim, grants **no** G001, no promotion, no fusion weight, and changes no config or `ACTIVE_VERSION`. It does not claim any prior finding is wrong; it claims the historical measurement object was narrower than production's, and names the kernel that closes the gap. Authority: architecture/governance only.


### F-089 · The parent-CRT bias gate is REACHABLE and it FIRES, but it is DECISION-NEUTRAL on XAUUSD — arming it changes the recorded REASON on 4 of 19 rejections and changes no outcome anywhere
- Type:          ARCHITECTURE
- Family:        RF-HTF
- Contract:      UNKNOWN
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Certain
- Validated:     2026-08-22
- Revalidate-by: 2027-02-18
- Evidence:      A natural A/B of exactly one gate. `parent_crt.enabled` is **true** on `ACTIVE_VERSION` = `configs/production/v2_htfcrt_2026_08.json` and **false** on `configs/production/v2_multi_2026_04.json`, while their `params` blocks are IDENTICAL (same `config_hash` `7de09f6233b712f0…` — `parent_crt` is a non-`params` section and therefore hash-neutral, §6.5). Both arms were run through the SAME governed registry loader the live path uses (`load_prod_config_from_registry`, `verify_hash=True`, the F-057 anti-split-brain discipline), on the Phase-1 frozen XAUUSD M15 corpus (sha256 `4d73f5ce…`, 47,275 bars, 2024-05-22 → 2026-05-21). **Result — the gate fires and nothing moves:** the CRT state sequence (`STATE_TRANSITION` + `RESET`, **n=5,269**) is **byte-identical**, sha `9dc22c12a9544c94` in both arms (the digest the probe emits; an earlier hand-computed `4f09654ac8f50700` hashed a tuple that also carried `candle_index` — same equality, different digest, and the reproducible one is cited); the per-kind event profile is identical across all 10 event kinds; trades are identical (3 `TRADE_OPENED`, 1 TP1, 1 TP2, 2 stopped); the `FILTER_REJECTED` timestamp SET is identical (19 vs 19, none present in only one arm). **Exactly 4 rejections differ in `reason` only** — `2025-01-31T21:00`, `2025-07-10T20:15`, `2026-02-19T11:30`, `2026-03-18T11:30`, each moving from `off_session:OFF_SESSION` to `Against parent-timeframe bias (LONG|SHORT)`. Every candidate the parent gate vetoes was **already** being vetoed by the off-session filter, so the gate changes the recorded reason and never the decision. **Non-vacuity holds** (the F-070 guard): the gate is not silent — it fired 4 times — so this is *measured neutrality*, not an untested gate. Reproducible probe: `scripts/research/parent_crt_pivotality_probe.py` (OBSERVATION_ONLY; re-runs BOTH arms itself rather than diffing gitignored run directories, so it reproduces from a clean clone), floor `tests/test_parent_crt_pivotality.py` (20 tests, including cases proving the probe *can* return `PIVOTAL` and `VACUOUS` — per E-001, a test that cannot fail is not enforcement). State-track construction (both event families; the engine emits `STATE_TRANSITION` into RANGE **zero** times and returns to RANGE only via `RESET`) lives in `src/charts/crt_overlay.py`. Gate implementation: `src/config_layer/parent_crt.py`.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — **Refines F-075, reverses nothing.** F-075 recorded the gate as built, armed on the active config, and *reachable* via `BacktestRunner` threading `ParentCRTFeed.bias`. All of that stands and is re-confirmed here. F-089 adds the next question F-075 did not ask: reachable and firing is not the same as pivotal. `REACHABLE != PIVOTAL`, the same distinction F-036 drew for the zone gate (tunable but ΔG001 ≡ 0) and F-070 for the 4-engine fusion gate (vetoes 0/30).
- Owner:         claude (registered 2026-08-22 under a user-approved research/governance-only plan)
- Note:          **AN E-001 CORRECTION IS EMBEDDED IN THIS FINDING'S OWN EVIDENCE.** The first statement of this result claimed the two configs "differ ONLY in `parent_crt.enabled`". That was measured with too coarse an instrument — top-level KEY SETS + `params` + `config_hash` — and is FALSE: they differ in **12** top-level sections. Eleven are provenance or comment fields (`config_id`, `created_at`, `promoted_at`, `notes`, `version`, `validation_summary`, and four `_comment*`/`note` strings, one of which differs only by a mojibake em-dash). The twelfth is real and had to be adjudicated: `ultron_risk_gate` on the ON config DECLARES the four F-082 cost-tax keys (`spread_pips` 0.0, `slippage_pips` 0.0, `pip_size` 0.0001, `min_sl_pips` 0.0) which are simply ABSENT on the OFF config. Source-verified inert, not assumed: each declared value equals the in-code default exactly (`src/core/ultron_risk_gate.py`, the `self.config.get(...)` defaults) and each consumer sits behind a `> 0` guard that is false in BOTH arms (`total_cost_pips > 0` for the cost tax; `min_sl_pips > 0` for the FRAG-6 floor). The probe therefore does not assert an empty diff — it enumerates every exemption with its evidence in `_ADJUDICATED_INERT`, and the allowlist is **value-specific**, so `spread_pips: 0.0` is exempt while `spread_pips: 1.5` invalidates the whole comparison. An unexplained behavioural difference yields `INVALID_AB` and no verdict. **SCOPE — no overclaim.** This is XAUUSD M15 on ONE frozen corpus under these two config objects. It is **not** a claim that the gate is inert on any other instrument, corpus, or config, and **not** a recommendation to disable `parent_crt` — with n=4 firings there is no economic content here whatsoever, and a corpus whose candidates are not already off-session could plausibly make the same gate bind. Grants **no** authority (§6.5): no promotion, no G001, no fusion weight, no config change; `ACTIVE_VERSION` is untouched and the probe writes no config. **RESIDUE (out of scope, flagged not fixed):** two of the four gate firings are at 11:30 broker time yet are classified `off_session` in the OFF arm — worth a look against F-066 (53.36% of XAUUSD session labels are wrong on the uncorrected broker basis), but the session filter is deliberately left on broker time per F-066 and re-labelling it is a separate economic decision. Also observed while gathering evidence: `configs/production/v2_htfcrt_2026_08.json`, the ACTIVE production config, is **not tracked by git** — the F-071 class (committed repository != running system) recurring on a config created after that restore. Authority: architecture/governance only.

Floors: `tests/research/test_context_attribution_contract.py`, `tests/test_bar_structure_snapshot.py`, `tests/governance/test_bar_structure_grounding.py`, `tests/test_smc_break_event_memo.py`. Binding failure is the `long_only` control (holdout −0.1731R, train −0.2935R) against every |delta_within_stratum| in 0.002–0.064: **0/22 beat controls**. Min p=0.015 vs the BH rank-1 threshold 0.00455 at q=0.10 → **0/22 survive BH-FDR**. Sign match 16/22 — sign stability alone would not have discriminated; the controls did.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — Does not reopen F-086 (different question: incremental value ORTHOGONAL to CRT state; a null here is consistent with, not additional to, F-086's 0-of-84). Does not retune SEM-012/026/028/030/031. Does not touch a production config. Does not add a canonical dimension.

- Note:          **THE DIAGNOSTIC IS THE RESULT.** `crt_proxy_component = delta_marginal − delta_within_stratum` shows the apparent effects ARE the stratum: `htf|short` marginal +0.0756 of which +0.0639 (85%) is CRT proxy; **`htf|long` flips sign entirely** (marginal +0.0247 → within-stratum −0.0207). An uncontrolled version of this study would have reported HTF/objective agreement as a discovery. **METRIC CORRECTION, pre-registered:** the primary metric was tightened to stratum-CENTERED y with non-varying strata skipped, BEFORE any y was computed — uncentered, the delta retains a `k·stratum_mean·(n_a−n_d)/n` term, and at a −0.29R base rate an information-free context with lopsided cells scores a spurious effect of the size being hunted. Caught by the orthogonality floor on a planted fixture. **SCOPE:** every-bar population, NOT production's executed trades (the CRT ledger is n=3 on this corpus — which is why the population had to be inverted at all). Effective independent n ≈941/direction (F-086 measured), not 94,314. Selecting bars by realised outcome is lookahead by construction (SEM-018). Composite family is a function of the other ten and is counted inside the 22, not as extra evidence. mt00 PARTIAL (14 PASS / 0 FAIL / 2 structurally INCONCLUSIVE); mt01 UNRUN 0/27 → E4 unmet. `economic_claims_allowed` false. `context_attribution.promoted_families` stays empty. No G001. No production.

- Evidence:      Pre-registered BEFORE any live snapshot existed: `docs/research/preregistration-bc2-open-close-label.md` (sections 1-8 frozen 2026-09-02; section 9 amendments 2026-09-03). Scorer `src/research/ohlcv_open_close_label.py`, floor `tests/test_ohlcv_open_close_label.py` (20 tests). Live pair + verdict: `docs/research-readiness/bc2_open_close/README.md`. **Verdict `OPEN`**, `executable_open_vs_close_label_proof: true` — the artifact recorded ABSENT in the Phase-1 G2 residual. Bar T = 2026-09-02T22:30:00 broker. Snapshot A at 22:32:13 (delta +133s, inside the forming bar); snapshot B at 22:46:44 (B's newest bar is 22:45, so T had closed). T mutated high 4382.83 -> 4388.71, close 4382.12 -> 4388.12, volume 1117 -> 7478, while **`open` stayed 4381.17 in both** — the open-label signature. Independent consistency check: 1117 of an eventual 7478 (~15%) at 133s of a 900s bar (14.8%). Terminal `ICMarketsSC-Demo` build 6140; clock `mt5_tick`, `clock_basis: broker_local`, skew -10799s (= UTC+3, a HEALTHY offset under F-066, not staleness).
- Supersedes:    —

Before this, that prior was belief: the closure report lists G-06 under UNPROVEN and the Phase-1 residual recorded `executable_open_vs_close_label_proof = ABSENT`. Does not reverse F-066 — F-066 is about WHICH CLOCK the stamp is on (broker-server, not UTC); this is about WHICH EDGE of the bar it names. The two are orthogonal and both hold.
- Owner:         claude
- Note:          **SCOPE — closure is boundary-scoped and non-transitive.** G-06 flips for the **mt5 acquisition family only**. `OHLCV_CLOSURE_STATUS` stays `BLOCKED:BC-1,BC-2[binance|yfinance],BC-3,BC-4,BC-5,BC-6[yfinance]`. No APPROVED, no R3b, no G001, no dataset-record edit, no fetcher change. **`producer_family_match` is `UNVERIFIED_NO_RECORDED_TERMINAL`**: the frozen dataset record `docs/governance/datasets/XAUUSD_MT5_PHASE1_20260521.json` carries no terminal identity, so this proves the label convention of the mt5 PRODUCER FAMILY — not that this terminal is the one that wrote `4d73f5ce...`. The inference to the admitted artifact is producer identity, per prereg section 2. **EMISSION DEFECTS FIXED BEFORE SCORING, deliberately:** a trace found the verdict logic rule-parity-clean against the frozen section 5, but the report layer would have contaminated this artifact — `grants_g06_mt5` was `true` on the CLOSE branch (G-06 IS open-time labeling, so CLOSE contradicts it); the capture-failure path emitted 3 of 12 keys; `admitted_sha256` was asserted but never checked (now verified, match `true`); and a synthetic fixture scored identically to a live one, so "synthetic never flips governance" was prose with no mechanism (now `source` fail-closed). Same silent-gap class as F-079 / F-083 / F-085. **ONE NEW PRECONDITION** (prereg section 9.1): a stale-feed guard at 6h, deliberately offset-tolerant because a healthy MT5 terminal legitimately reads UTC+2/+3 (F-066) — one-directional, it may only downgrade to INSUFFICIENT and can never manufacture a hit. **OPERATIONAL HAZARD RECORDED:** the first `copy_rates_from_pos` after `symbol_select` returned a cold cache 5099s behind the live tick, and a capture at delta=884s missed the 840s window edge — both score `not_mid_interval`, a correct rejection indistinguishable from a closed market.
