### F-099 · MT5 `volume` counts something tick-adjacent (H_REAL rejected, artifact fully bound over 2 years) but does not exact-match COPY_TICKS_ALL — TICK_VOLUME_APPROXIMATE, not CONFIRMED
- Type:          GOVERNANCE
- Family:        — (not a market object; see docs/governance/research_family_registry.json)
- Contract:      UNKNOWN
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Likely
- Validated:     2026-09-03
- Revalidate-by: 2026-12-02
- Evidence:      Pre-registered BEFORE any live data beyond the Phase 0 feasibility gate was scored: `docs/research/preregistration-bc4-volume-semantics.md`. Scorer `src/research/ohlcv_volume_semantics.py` + shared emission module `src/research/ohlcv_probe_report.py` (extracted from the BC-2 scorer, BC-2 left byte-identical). Floor `tests/test_ohlcv_volume_semantics.py` (16 tests). Live evidence: `docs/research-readiness/bc4_volume_semantics/`. **Phase 0 feasibility gate (binding, recorded before Test 1 ran):** `copy_ticks_range` returns immediately for windows within ~1h of capture time (8,117-9,535 ticks) but HANGS INDEFINITELY (no error, no return) for windows 3h+ in the past — observed at both 3h and 1-day marks, 130s-165s+ waits with no response. This bounds Test 1 to bars closed within the last 60 minutes; it does not affect Test 2, which uses only `copy_rates_range` (M15 bars). **Test 1** (4 fresh closed bars, `tick_volume` vs independently-counted `COPY_TICKS_ALL`): diffs `45,46,52,31` on `tick_volume` `6654,7478,9483,4924` — a consistent 0.55-0.68% gap, `ticks_all` always >= `tick_volume`, never an exact match (0/4). `real_volume` was `0` on all 4 bars while `tick_volume>0` -> `H_REAL` REJECTED for this family (the frozen CSV has no `real_volume` column; this evidence only exists because of the live probe). Per the frozen prereg's own rule (small directional gap != exact match), verdict is `TICK_VOLUME_APPROXIMATE`, a DISTINCT outcome from `TICK_VOLUME_CONFIRMED` — not rounded up. **Test 2** (20 bars sampled across the frozen corpus's full 2024-05-22 -> 2026-05-21 range, re-fetched live via `copy_rates_range` and compared to the frozen CSV's `volume` column): 17/20 resolved (3 gaps consistent with session/holiday closures, same class as F-098's documented gaps), **17/17 exact match -> `artifact_binding = BOUND`**.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — Does not close the BC-4b residual (`docs/governance/CORPUS_AUTHORITY.md:108`) the way a `CONFIRMED` verdict would; the closure report's own interpretation table (prereg section 7) reserves closure for `CONFIRMED`. Does not reverse F-098 (BC-2) — this probe CONSUMES that result (the tick window `[T, T+15m)` is only well-defined because F-098 proved T is the bar open) rather than re-deriving it. Does not touch `producer_family_match` (BC-2's `UNVERIFIED_NO_RECORDED_TERMINAL`) for terminal identity in general, though `artifact_binding=BOUND` narrows it for the volume COLUMN specifically.
- Owner:         claude
- Note:          **THREE BUGS FOUND AND FIXED DURING CAPTURE, before any verdict was scored — all in the capture layer, none in the frozen scoring rules.** (1) Test 1's freshness `age` check compared TRUE wall clock (`datetime.now(UTC)`) against nominal broker-labeled bar timestamps (F-066: MT5 epochs are broker-server seconds mislabeled UTC) -- at the measured UTC+3 skew every bar looked ~3h in the future, so `age<0` excluded all 4 bars on the first capture attempt (0/4 scored). Fixed by comparing against `fetched_at` (same nominal clock basis as the bar timestamps), not true wall clock. (2) Test 2's `copy_rates_range` call was missing its required `timeframe` positional argument (`mt5.TIMEFRAME_M15`) -- silently returned `None` for all 20 samples on the first attempt, easily misread as "history unavailable" (a direct read-only probe confirmed history DOES reach back to 2024-05-22, ruling that out before touching the code). (3) Test 2's sample-target generation used raw `step * i` arithmetic that produced fractional-second timestamps never aligned to the M15 lattice (19/20 landed off-lattice); fixed with an explicit lattice-snap (`epoch -= epoch % 900`) before both the fetch and the match. Each fix was diagnosed with a direct, isolated read-only probe rather than guessed at. **SCOPE:** `TICK_VOLUME_APPROXIMATE` is real, structured evidence (a consistent ~0.6% directional gap across 4 independent bars, not noise) and a genuine advance -- `H_REAL` rejected and `artifact_binding` BOUND at 100% over the full 2-year corpus are both decisive -- but it is NOT the `CONFIRMED` verdict the pre-registration reserved for full closure, and is reported as such rather than rounded up. BC-4 stays a blocker in `OHLCV_CLOSURE_STATUS`. BC-4a (schema enforcement: wiring the already-defined `volume_semantic` enum via the currently-dead `SCHEMA_PATH`, adding `is_synthetic`, writing SEED-OHLCV-19) is a separate, still-open half of the same program. No APPROVED, no R3b, no G001, no dataset-record edit, no fetcher rewrite.

### F-100 · The BC-4 tick-volume residual is UNATTRIBUTED — a perfect 4/4 discovery pattern failed out-of-sample, and the MT5 tick FLAG VOCABULARY is not stable across sessions
- Type:          GOVERNANCE
- Family:        — (not a market object; see docs/governance/research_family_registry.json)
- Contract:      UNKNOWN
- Status:        VALIDATED
- Confidence:    Likely
- Validated:     2026-09-03
- Revalidate-by: 2026-12-02
- Evidence:      Pre-registered `docs/research/preregistration-bc4-residual-attribution.md` (probe `BC4-RESIDUAL-ATTRIBUTION-XAUUSD-MT5-V1`); candidate list frozen in the user-approved plan BEFORE any raw tick array existed, section 8 amendment appended BEFORE the holdout was scored. Code `src/research/ohlcv_tick_attribution.py`, floor `tests/test_ohlcv_tick_attribution.py` (18 tests). Evidence `docs/research-readiness/bc4_residual_attribution/` (`discovery.json` fetched_at 2026-09-02T23:52:23, `holdout.json` fetched_at 2026-09-03T08:10:32, zero bar overlap, `independent_captures: true`). **V1: `UNATTRIBUTED`** — 0 of 9 frozen candidates matched every discovery bar. **V2: `UNATTRIBUTED_V2`** — the discovery-generated hypothesis `C9_flag_bid = count(flags & TICK_FLAG_BID)` matched discovery **4/4 exactly** (9422+61=9483, 4878+46=4924, 4231+55=4286, 2508+23=2531) and then **failed the independent holdout**: 3 of 4 exact, but the 07:00 bar gave 5437 vs tick_volume 5466 (delta −29, and in the OPPOSITE direction to discovery, where the raw count always OVERshot). **Mechanism of the failure, recorded descriptively (no third search — prereg 8.3 forbids it):** the tick flag vocabulary differs between sessions. Discovery bars carried `{134, 130, 4}` (the `128` bit set on nearly every tick); holdout bars 07:15/07:30/07:45 carried `{6, 2, 4}` (no `128` bit at all); and the single failing 07:00 bar carried **both at once** (`134:3522, 6:1838, 4:63, 130:46, 2:31`) — an encoding transition spanning one bar. On the three pure-vocabulary holdout bars C9 matched exactly; it failed only on the transition bar.
- Supersedes:    —
- Reversal:      — Does not reverse F-099. F-099's `TICK_VOLUME_APPROXIMATE` stands unchanged, and BC-4b stays `PARTIALLY_ADVANCED`; this program tried and failed to explain its residual, which leaves the residual measured but unexplained. Does not touch F-098 (BC-2), which this program consumes (the `[T, T+15m)` window is well-defined only because T is proven to be the bar open).
- Owner:         claude
- Note:          **THE HOLDOUT REQUIREMENT PREVENTED A FALSE CLAIM, demonstrably.** Discovery was a perfect 4-of-4 exact integer match on values 2531–9483; on that evidence alone the natural conclusion is "tick_volume counts BID-flagged ticks, attribution complete." The out-of-sample capture refuted it. The single-session alternative was explicitly considered and declined before any data was seen; had it been taken, this finding would read ATTRIBUTED and be wrong. **DISCIPLINE APPLIED:** the pre-registered `C4_flag_bid_or_ask` (BID|ASK) failed because it also counts bare-ASK ticks; the surviving pattern was BID-only — one bit different from what was frozen. That one bit is exactly why V1 could not claim it: the hypothesis was generated by looking at discovery output, so it was registered as a hypothesis with zero out-of-sample support and tested separately (V2), rather than retro-fitted into V1. `C9_flag_bid` is deliberately kept OUT of the V1 `CANDIDATES` dict so V1's `UNATTRIBUTED` stays byte-reproducible (pinned by `test_v2_candidate_is_not_in_the_v1_frozen_list`). **CONSEQUENCE FOR BC-5:** BC-5 remains blocked. The residual is now better characterised than before — bounded at 0.55–0.68%, demonstrably NOT explained by tick class (`COPY_TICKS_INFO == COPY_TICKS_ALL`, `COPY_TICKS_TRADE == 0`), boundary inclusion, exact duplication, price-change filtering, or BID-flag filtering — but it is not explained. **NEW RISK SURFACED:** any future attribution attempt must treat the tick flag vocabulary as non-stationary; a rule fitted on one session's encoding can fail on another's, which is precisely the failure observed. No APPROVED, no R3b, no G001, no dataset-record edit, no production config.

## Funding Ledger (initiative-level capital allocation)

> A **finding** is an observation; **funding** is a capital-allocation decision derived from
> findings — and one finding (e.g. F-001) kills or funds many initiatives. This ledger keeps the
> two adjacent but separate. **Never silently revive a `KILLED`/`FROZEN` initiative:** its
> `Reopen Conditions` must be met *and* a SESSION LOG entry filed (per `CLAUDE.md §6.2`).
> Status vocab: `FUNDED · FROZEN · KILLED · RESEARCH · UNFUNDED`. `Evidence` cites finding IDs
> (CI checks they resolve); `FROZEN`/`KILLED` require non-empty `Reopen Conditions`.

### Dynamic Stop-Policy Optimization (XAUUSD M15) — KILLED
- Date:    2026-08-21
- Evidence: F-087 (the whole 12-policy axis spans 0.33–0.49 of ONE confidence interval at three horizons independently, ~1/12th of the stop-WIDTH axis; the extreme-following half of the grid carries a same-bar ambiguity band 9–15× the spread it would measure, so it is unrankable from M15 OHLC at all; 0 of 16 paired tests find interaction with the entry information), F-086 (the entry information it would consume does not clear zero), F-025 (independent corroboration on crypto that exit/cost is a risk lever, not expectancy)
- Reopen Conditions: BOTH of (a) tick or sub-M15 data is available for the instrument under test, so the extreme-following arms (`ratchet_*`, `breakeven_*`) become rankable at all rather than being drowned by a 0.261R mean ambiguity band (sharpened 2026-08-21 from a 0.196R all-arms mean that included the 4 non-governed `time_decay` control arms); AND (b) an entry population exists whose GROSS expectancy is demonstrably above zero under a sealed measurement contract — an exit cannot create expectancy the entry did not contain, so any policy search over a gross-zero population is re-deciding how much of a zero-mean quantity becomes cost. Absent both, do NOT re-run the 390-cell geometry × policy sweep; the answer is recorded. Note that stop **WIDTH** is explicitly NOT killed by this entry — it moves net by 4–6 CI widths, but it does so through `cost_r = cost_price / risk_distance` rather than through skill, so it is a cost/risk lever governed by `MEASUREMENT_CONTRACT.md`, not a policy-search initiative.

### Cross-Instrument Decontamination of the Outcome-First Oracle (EURUSD M15) — UNFUNDED
- Date:    2026-08-21
- Evidence: F-086 (the outcome-first instrument exists and its 5-check harness gate passes, but every reading is on ONE drifting instrument), F-087 (gold's drift contaminated three separate readings — tightening stops helps shorts and hurts longs, mistaken for a policy effect once and caught only by testing across all groups), F-035 (the prior FX cost caveat: flat 12bps is 1.8–2.5× the median FX M15 bar), F-082 (flat bps was ~11× too punitive on XAUUSD — the defect these programs corrected)
- Reopen Conditions: n/a — designed, not funded; no reopen gate required. **Design (ready to execute, nothing built needed):** every stage is already instrument-parameterised (`--instrument` / `--csv`), and `data/mt5/EURUSD_M15.csv` is on disk, so this is a re-run, not a build — `scripts/research/build_bar_matrix.py` → `scripts/research/validate_oracle_harness.py` (the 5 blocking checks must pass on the NEW corpus before anything is measured; a null from a broken detector is indistinguishable from a null from an efficient market) → `research.oracle.labeler` → `scripts/research/oracle_pattern_scan.py` → `scripts/research/exit_geometry_scan.py --stage ceilings|sweep|interaction`. **BLOCKING PREREQUISITE, named rather than worked around:** these programs price trades with `ComponentCostModel` (SEM-015), which is fail-closed and instrument-scoped (`src/research/costs.py` `from_manifest(..., instrument=...)` raises on missing components or a non-usable status). The only measured calibration on disk is `results/research/xauusd_mt5_cost_calibration/` and the only calibration script is `scripts/research/xauusd_mt5_cost_calibration.py` — XAUUSD-named and requiring a live MT5 connection; `configs/research/measurement_contracts/fx_majors.v1.json` exists but is DRAFT. So an EURUSD run **cannot produce a net-basis number** until an FX calibration is captured. Reverting to flat bps is NOT an acceptable substitute (F-035/F-082). Two admissible paths: (a) capture the FX calibration first and run on the net basis, or (b) scope the run to GROSS basis only and declare that in the write-up. **Two adjacent decisions, both surfaced by F-086 and both recorded as taken/not-taken here:** the permutation count may be raised from 3 to 19 on the existing XAUUSD scan (`--permutation-null 19`, matching the exit program's interaction floor of p=0.05, written to a DISTINCT `--out-dir` so F-086's cited `scan_report_train.json` is not overwritten) — cheap and it sharpens only the beats-zero count; and the 236-bar non-overlapping holdout (`--split test_stride`) is recommended to be LEFT UNSPENT, because it is one-shot, thin for an n≈60 conjunction that already fails to clear zero on TRAIN, and a second instrument is the better source of power.

- Evidence: F-027 (3A verdict: 0 PROMOTE at H1 and H4); F-019, F-020, F-021, F-025 (Program 1's four-falsification closure scoped the kill to *next-bar · M15 · crypto-majors* and recorded an UNTOUCHED FRONTIER — H1/H4/D horizons among it — as the sanctioned reopen path; docs/analysis/program-1-closure-2026-06-13.md §"EXHAUSTED ≠ WRONG")
- Hypothesis (pre-registered, BEFORE running): the directional null (F-019/020/021/025) is specific to the M15 horizon's signal-to-noise; on a coarser bar the SAME hypothesis pool (toy expansion_breakout/mean_reversion + the production spine) may clear the M4 gate. NONE was implied to contain edge.
- Design: deterministic M15→{H1,H4} resampler (src/research/resample.py, calendar-boundary, causal trailing-drop, byte-identical/SHA-stable — tests/research/test_resample.py) feeds the EXISTING audited spine VERBATIM (forward_walk intrabar_fixed + CostModel 12bps + IS/70-30 OOS + BH; research.qualification). Drivers: scripts/research/build_resampled_data.py + scripts/research/qualify_htf.py. Configs: configs/research/research_config_htf_majors.json + research_config_spine_htf_majors.json.
- Horizon split (avoids the two-questions trap): 3A = BAR_COUNT_CONSTANT (PRIMARY, RUN — FALSIFIED) — harness bar-counts held constant (max_forward=40 = 10h M15 / 40h H1 / 160h H4); the null reads as "this bar-relative ontology failed." 3B = WALL_CLOCK_CONSTANT (CONTINGENT, pre-defined NOT built) — disambiguates horizon length from timeframe structure if ever warranted.
- Verdict (3A): toy directional pool 0 PROMOTE at H1 and H4 (F-027); spine arm non-decisive (H1 n≤10 INSUFFICIENT; H4 NOT_MEASURABLE — adapter index contract). Coarser timeframes are not the missing lever.
- Reopen Conditions: ONLY (a) Program 3B (WALL_CLOCK_CONSTANT) showing a candidate clear the M4 gate IS&OOS at a wall-clock-matched horizon, OR (b) a genuinely NEW ontology (non-directional target — vol/range/persistence — or a non-crypto universe — FX/metals/equities; data present in data/) with a pre-registered hypothesis. NOT reopenable by any Program-1 parameter pass (no SL/TP/session/entropy/score tweaks, no re-running 3A with tuned knobs) — that is archaeology.

## Research Envelope / Scope Matrix

> **What this is.** The scannable answer to "is the paradigm dead, or was the experiment too
> narrow?" Each falsification (F-019…F-026) is a verdict *within a measured envelope*; this matrix
> makes the envelope — and the **untouched frontier** — explicit so a null is never over-read as a
> global claim. Authoritative on conflict = the finding rows above + the Program-1 closure
> (docs/analysis/program-1-closure-2026-06-13.md). `Updated: 2026-06-13`.
>
> Status vocab: `FALSIFIED-IN-SCOPE` (null under the governing truth standard, scope stated) ·
> `ALREADY-ANSWERED` (a frequently-proposed "unknown" that the repo has in fact measured) ·
> `UNTOUCHED-FRONTIER` (not yet tested; a legitimate reopen axis — a NEW program, not archaeology).

| Axis | Tested envelope | Status | Evidence / note |
|---|---|---|---|
| Entry edge (next-bar direction) | crypto-majors · **M15** · intrabar_fixed+12bps · IS+70/30 OOS | FALSIFIED-IN-SCOPE | F-019 (toys ≈ random on all 4 majors + pooled; spine throughput-starved) |
| Conditional direction (session×vol×momentum) | crypto-majors · M15 · h≤20 · paired entropy+economics | FALSIFIED-IN-SCOPE | F-020 (25/25 entropy-"significant" at N but 0 economic pockets) |
| Selection skill (RETEST selected−rejected) | crypto-6 · M15 · decomposed by reject-reason | FALSIFIED-IN-SCOPE | F-021 (the +1.17R ΔE is ENTIRELY the incumbent SESSION filter; ZONE/SCORE null) |
| Exit/cost geometry | crypto-6 · M15 · **42-cell SL{0.5–3.0}×TP{1.0–5.0}** grid, entries fixed | ALREADY-ANSWERED | F-025 (every cell E_oos<0 incl. 3.0×5.0; "widen the stop to 2.5 ATR" is *inside* this grid — re-running is Program-1 archaeology, out-of-bounds) |
